# C72: full primitive cone, a lossless optimum lift, and the exact reuse threshold

Status: self-contained research draft, UNVERIFIED. Sixth launch, first round,
cumulative round 14. No verifier call is authorized. All new results are proved
jointly here; shared findings are reference evidence, not predecessors.
Accepted background: b98bb807d514e80d (benchmark definitions),
0be8b54e0a33381c (square-root mass contraction), and 1252b8823bb5a290
(full smooth-convex interpolation and its explicit conjugate interpolant).

## 1. The intrinsic property and every original vector

Normalize L=1. For a nonnegative word h of length n let s=sum h_i,
H_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t, and

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
 V_h=2sum_(i<n)h_i(f_i-f_n)+sum_(i<n)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
                                      -s(s+1)||g_n||^2.

Call h primitive if V_h>=0 on every actual smooth convex run. This is exactly
Zhang--Jiang's primitive property: expansion of their difference of squared
positions cancels x_0 and all mixed gradient terms and leaves V_h. No norm
of x_0 is being substituted for the original objective distance.

Let e denote the n+1 column of ones; define A_ii=1, A_it=h_t for t<i,
and A_it=0 for t>i. The formal gradients g_0,...,g_n are independent.
Choose ALL n(n+1) ordered finite multipliers lambda_ij>=0, i!=j; their
zero diagonal is conventional. Define L=diag(lambda e)-lambda and

 omega=(-2h_0,...,-2h_(n-1),2s)^T,
 D=diag(h_0(2-h_0),...,h_(n-1)(2-h_(n-1)),-s(s+2)).

Then the full sufficient cone is exactly

 L^T e=omega,                 M=D+(LA+A^T L^T)/2 >=0.            (1)

Indeed value cancellation in V_h+sum lambda Q is the displayed flow.
The position terms have matrix sym(L(A-I)); the squared-difference terms
have matrix sym(L)-diag(omega)/2. Adding V's diagonal gives (1), with

                  V_h+sum lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g)>.          (2)

Thus (1) implies V_h>=0 because Q_ij<=0. All incoming terminal edges are
present. There is no rank, symmetry, child, sparsity, or fixed-remainder
restriction on lambda or M. In particular accepted terminal reduction
1252b8823bb5a290 is NOT used to delete primitive constraints.

Adding optimum indices to this certificate cannot enlarge its feasible
cone. With x_star=g_star=f_star=0, the x_0 squared coefficient remains
zero. Its mixed coefficient with g_i is -lambda_(i,star). A PSD matrix with
a zero diagonal entry has that entire row zero (test the corresponding
2x2 principal minors). Therefore all lambda_(i,star)=0. The sum of finite
value-flow equations is then -sum lambda_(star,i)=0, since the V value
coefficients sum to zero. Nonnegativity forces all lambda_(star,i)=0.
Consequently the full optimum/position column is forced to be identically
zero; (1) preserves the entire remaining cone, not a guessed reduction.

A unit-slope Huber run started beyond 1+s has g_i=1, Q_ij=0, and V_h=0.
For any certificate (1) this gives e^T M e=0 and hence M e=0. This is an
EXACT equality kernel, never a positive full-space eigenvalue margin. The
numerical formulation below uses the full matrix without a kernel quotient.

## 2. Lossless intrinsic primal: explicitly restore a minimizer

For arbitrary finite sample jets obeying every Q_ij<=0 and the GD recursion,
there is a legal smooth convex function with a minimizer and the SAME V_h.
This justifies the intrinsic model at the level of actual functions as well
as the preceding full-certificate column argument.

Fix epsilon>0 and append one coordinate. Put

 g'_i=(g_i,epsilon), x'_i=(x_i,T-epsilon H_i),
 f'_i=f_i+c-epsilon^2 H_i.

The GD recursion remains exact, and direct substitution shows Q'_ij=Q_ij:
the extra function difference -epsilon^2(H_i-H_j) cancels the extra
position term epsilon^2(H_i-H_j). Choose the finite constants

 c>=max_i{-f_i+epsilon^2 H_i+(||g_i||^2+epsilon^2)/2},
 epsilon T>=max_i{f_i+c-<g_i,x_i>+(||g_i||^2+epsilon^2)/2}.

After adding the optimum jet (0,0,0), these are exactly Q'_(star,i)<=0
and Q'_(i,star)<=0. The full conjugate interpolation construction in
accepted fact1252b8823bb5a290 supplies an everywhere finite convex
1-smooth interpolant with this optimum and every displayed jet. Thus it
is a genuine same-function GD run with an actual minimizer.

The additional V contribution is epsilon^2 times

 2sum_i h_i(s-H_i)+sum_i h_i(1-h_i)-s(s+1)=0,

because 2sum_i h_i(s-H_i)=s^2+sum_i h_i^2. Hence V'_h=V_h exactly.
This lift does NOT preserve the original distance or normalized objective,
and is used ONLY for intrinsic primitive validity. Zero steps and n=0
cause no exception; identical original queries stay identical after a zero
step. All formal original gradients remain in the final cone model.

It follows that minimizing V_h over the full Gram G>=0, trace(G)=1, all
ordered Q_ij<=0, and a harmless value gauge f_n=0 detects primitive failure
exactly at the sign level. Every negative feasible V yields a legal failure
by the lift. Conversely every nonzero-gradient legal failure can be divided
by its positive total squared gradient to meet trace(G)=1. Zero-gradient
jets cannot have negative V, since all values then agree. A common-gradient
Huber transcript has V=0, so this minimum is always <=0.

For positive steps the primal also has strict feasibility: use a diagonal
quadratic with n+1 distinct curvatures in (0,1) and nonzero initial
coordinates, then scale to trace(G)=1. The gradient polynomial columns
z product_(t<i)(1-h_t z) have distinct degrees 1,...,n+1 and are independent
at n+1 nonzero distinct nodes (divide by z). Every finite distinct-query
residual is strictly negative. This is the same Vandermonde argument as in
accepted1252b8823bb5a290. Values are bounded after fixing f_n because the
two ordered residuals bound every f_i-f_n and trace(G)=1 bounds every Gram
entry. Thus no unbounded objective is concealed by the gauge.

## 3. Composition identities, including arbitrary PSD child remainders

These identities are rederived from the Q definition and coincide with the
proofs of Zhang--Jiang, arXiv:2410.12395v2, Theorems3.1/3.2. Their exact
local paper, definitions, and both proofs were reread. No external result
with unexamined hypotheses is needed for the algebra below.

Primitive join. Let a,b be primitive, A=1+sum a, C=1+sum b. Let d>0 solve

 d(A+C+d)=AC; T=A+C+d, kappa=(T/C)^2.

Form h=(a,1+d,b). Let p be the bridge query and z the terminal query, and
let j range over the nonterminal queries in b. Direct expansion gives

 V_h=V_a+kappa V_b-(kappa-1)[sum_j b_j Q_(j,p)+Q_(z,p)
                                  +d Q_(p,z)+sum_j b_j Q_(j,z)]. (3)

One way to audit all coefficients is to call the bracket C_aux; its value
part is 2sum_j b_j f_j+(d-C)f_p+(2-C-d)f_z, and its gradient part is

 sum_j b_j(1-b_j)||g_j||^2+[(C-d)/2-d^2]||g_p||^2
                                          +(C+d)||g_z||^2/2.

Insert these in (3), and use (kappa-1)(C-d)=2(A+d), kappa d^2=A^2,
kappa C^2=T^2. The mixed terms cancel. Since C>d and kappa>1, every
bracket coefficient is nonnegative. Child certificates with matrices M_a,
M_b produce the FULL parent remainder M_a+kappa M_b on disjoint original
child-gradient sets. They may have arbitrary PSD rank. Its mass is

 T=Omega(A,C)=(A+C+sqrt((A+C)^2+4AC))/2.

Dominant join. Define, for weights u_i>=0 of mass D=2sum h+1,

 J_(h,u)=||x_0||^2+2sum_i u_i Q_(i,star)-2D f_z
                              -||x_0-sum_i u_i g_i||^2.

Dominance means J>=0 on every legal run; it implies W(h)<=1/D by Q_i*<=0.
The empty word has u=1 and J=0. A primitive word is dominant with
u=(h,s+1), because expanding the last square gives J=V_h exactly.
For a primitive left child of mass A, and a dominant right child of mass D
and weights v, put

 mu=2A/D, r=(1+sqrt(1+8A/D))/2, gamma=A+1, beta=1+A/r.

The parent word is (a,beta,b). Its weights are u=(a,gamma,r v), all
nonnegative, of total G(A,D)=2A+D/2+sqrt(D^2+8AD)/2=Dr^2. Its identity is

 J_parent=V_a+r^2 J_right-2mu sum_(i>p) v_i Q_(i,p).             (4)

To verify the equality expand both sides. The value flow uses
mu D=(sum a)+gamma=2A. The gradient coefficients reduce to
(sum a)(sum a+1)+mu D+gamma=gamma^2,
r^2=r+mu, and r^2 beta=mu+gamma r; these cancel every mixed term and
leave the defining parent square. This proves (4), including empty
children. It proves exactly the ConPD rule in accepted benchmark notation.

## 4. A finite seed threshold that compares the ORIGINAL exact B_n

Let lambda=1+sqrt(2), rho=log_2(lambda), q=1/rho. Suppose an actual
nonnegative primitive word a has length r_seed and A_seed=1+sum a. Put
m_seed=r_seed+1. The sufficient target condition is

                       A_seed>m_seed^rho.                      (5)

A gain merely above C_(r_seed) does not imply (5).
Here are the full amplification argument and the parameter losses.
First Omega(x,y)^q<=x^q+y^q for x,y>=0. For positive arguments use
x=exp(u-t), y=exp(u+t), t>=0. It becomes J(t)>=0 where

 J(t)=q^(-1)log(2cosh(qt))-arsinh(cosh t).

J(0)=0 and J(t) tends to zero at infinity. Its derivative has the sign
of qt-alpha(t), where alpha=artanh(sinh(t)/sqrt(1+cosh(t)^2)). Direct
differentiation gives alpha'=cosh(t)/sqrt(1+cosh(t)^2), strictly increasing
from 1/sqrt(2) to1. Also 1/sqrt(2)<q<1: lambda>2 and lambda^3<16 imply
1>q>3/4>1/sqrt(2). Thus alpha-qt starts by decreasing, has strictly
increasing derivative, and tends to infinity; it crosses zero once on
t>0. Therefore J first increases and then decreases to zero, proving the
inequality. Zero arguments follow directly from Omega(x,0)=x.

Induction on each primitive binary tree now gives C_k<= (k+1)^rho.
Unrolling a maximizing dominant tree, its primitive side branches have
leaf counts l_1,...,l_t summing to n. Since G(A,D)<=D+4A,

 B_n<=1+4sum l_i^rho<=1+4n^rho.

Balanced primitive trees give C_(2^k-1)=lambda^k, by the preceding upper
bound and Omega(A,A)=lambda A. Hence B_(2^k)>=G(lambda^k,1)>=2lambda^k.
The number K=limsup B_n/n^rho thus satisfies 2<=K<=4. No phase limit is
assumed and this is the ORIGINAL exact DP sequence.

Take a fully expanded optimizing dominant tree at length n, with smallest
maximizing split as deterministic tie rule. It has exactly n primitive
empty leaves and one final dominant empty leaf: induction on the unique
right dominant chain proves this count. Replace every primitive empty
leaf by a and recompute all physical bridges by (3)-(4). Leave the final
dominant empty leaf unchanged. The actual resulting word H_n has length
m_seed n and is dominant. Homogeneity of Omega makes each primitive mass
exactly A_seed times its former mass.

Let D_n=2sum H_n+1. In an auxiliary scalar evaluation also change the
terminal dominant mass from1 to A_seed. Homogeneity of G makes that
auxiliary root A_seed B_n. Accepted0be8b54e0a33381c proves
sqrt(G(A,d))=(sqrt(d)+sqrt(d+8A))/2 and that this is increasing and
1-Lipschitz as a function of sqrt(d). Propagating the unchanged terminal
leaf discrepancy therefore gives

 sqrt(A_seed B_n)-(sqrt(A_seed)-1)<=sqrt(D_n)<=sqrt(A_seed B_n). (6)

Choose n_j with B_(n_j)/n_j^rho tending to K. Equation(6) gives
D_(n_j)/n_j^rho tending to A_seed K, whereas by the SAME global limsup
limsup B_(m_seed n_j)/(m_seed n_j)^rho<=K. Consequently

 limsup_j B_(m_seed n_j) W_(m_seed n_j)(H_(n_j))
                           <= m_seed^rho/A_seed <1.            (7)

For delta=(1-m_seed^rho/A_seed)/2>0, every sufficiently large output
horizon in this subsequence has ratio <=1-delta. This is a fixed relative
all-function upper separation and suffices to refute the original uniform
asymptotic matching claim, conditional on the actual certified seed.
The unused dominant leaf and its O(sqrt(B_n)) mass loss are explicit.

## 5. Before-solve design, calibration signs, and scope

The new discovery lane searches length15 only, with every coordinate free
and fixed capacity A_target=(1+eta)lambda^4, initially eta=1/1000. The
known Silver word has length15 and exactly lambda^4 capacity, so (5) has
zero baseline threshold gap. Source order, unequal step magnitudes, and the
full all-pair sensitivity guide proposals; no symmetry or tensor class is
imposed. Length4 is used only for two exact model calibrations.

Calibration expected signs BEFORE any solve:
* h=(1,2,1,1) has min V=0. Its nonzero lambda entries are 01:2,10:4,
  21:3,31:3,41:3,14:3,23:8,32:16,34:35,43:48,24:3. Expansion gives
  M=|g0-g1|^2+4|g2-g3|^2+16|g3-g4|^2. Huber attains0.
* h=(3,1,1,1) is nonprimitive. Use x=(1,-2,-3/2,-1,-1/2),
  g=(1,-1/2,-1/2,-1/2,-1/2), f_i=g_i x_i-g_i^2/2. This is the
  convex1-smooth function with derivative clipped to[-1/2,1]. Direct
  substitution gives V=-45/4 and sum||g_i||^2=2. The trace-one model
  therefore has min V<=-45/8. Every actual query and optimum Q<=0.
* The full exact length15 Silver word has min V=0 by (3), starting with
  empty leaves. Its full nonnegative array is generated by (3), with
  identically zero PSD remainder. Its e kernel is not made strict.

The primal objective is NOT normalized objective error. Only an exactly
reconstructed nonnegative certificate satisfying (1) and (5), followed by
(3)-(7), could settle the original negative route. Solver feasibility,
positive residual tolerances, clipped eigenvalues, or a finite capacity
gain below (5) do not do so. All original statuses and raw primal/dual
arrays must be retained. The allowance is at most72 evaluations,120 measured
build-plus-solve seconds,n<=15,40 distinct nonbaseline words. No old budget
is renewed and no verifier call is made.

# C72: full primitive cone, a lossless optimum lift, and the exact reuse threshold

Status: self-contained research draft, UNVERIFIED. Sixth launch, first round,
cumulative round 14. No verifier call is authorized. All new results are proved
jointly here; shared findings are reference evidence, not predecessors.
Accepted background: b98bb807d514e80d (benchmark definitions),
0be8b54e0a33381c (square-root mass contraction), and 1252b8823bb5a290
(full smooth-convex interpolation and its explicit conjugate interpolant).

## 1. The intrinsic property and every original vector

Normalize L=1. For a nonnegative word h of length n let s=sum h_i,
P_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t, and

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
 V_h=2sum_(i<n)h_i(f_i-f_n)+sum_(i<n)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
                                      -s(s+1)||g_n||^2.

Call h primitive if V_h>=0 on every actual smooth convex run. This is exactly
Zhang--Jiang's primitive property: expansion of their difference of squared
positions cancels x_0 and all mixed gradient terms and leaves V_h. No norm
of x_0 is being substituted for the original objective distance.

Let e denote the n+1 column of ones; define A_ii=1, A_it=h_t for t<i,
and A_it=0 for t>i. The formal gradients g_0,...,g_n are independent.
Choose ALL n(n+1) ordered finite multipliers lambda_ij>=0, i!=j; their
zero diagonal is conventional. Define L=diag(lambda e)-lambda and

 omega=(-2h_0,...,-2h_(n-1),2s)^T,
 D=diag(h_0(2-h_0),...,h_(n-1)(2-h_(n-1)),-s(s+2)).

Then the full sufficient cone is exactly

 L^T e=omega,                 M=D+(LA+A^T L^T)/2 >=0.            (1)

Indeed value cancellation in V_h+sum lambda Q is the displayed flow.
The position terms have matrix sym(L(A-I)); the squared-difference terms
have matrix sym(L)-diag(omega)/2. Adding V's diagonal gives (1), with

                  V_h+sum lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g)>.          (2)

Thus (1) implies V_h>=0 because Q_ij<=0. All incoming terminal edges are
present. There is no rank, symmetry, child, sparsity, or fixed-remainder
restriction on lambda or M. In particular accepted terminal reduction
1252b8823bb5a290 is NOT used to delete primitive constraints.

Adding optimum indices to this certificate cannot enlarge its feasible
cone. With x_star=g_star=f_star=0, the x_0 squared coefficient remains
zero. Its mixed coefficient with g_i is -lambda_(i,star). A PSD matrix with
a zero diagonal entry has that entire row zero (test the corresponding
2x2 principal minors). Therefore all lambda_(i,star)=0. The sum of finite
value-flow equations is then -sum lambda_(star,i)=0, since the V value
coefficients sum to zero. Nonnegativity forces all lambda_(star,i)=0.
Consequently the full optimum/position column is forced to be identically
zero; (1) preserves the entire remaining cone, not a guessed reduction.

A unit-slope Huber run started beyond 1+s has g_i=1, Q_ij=0, and V_h=0.
For any certificate (1) this gives e^T M e=0 and hence M e=0. This is an
EXACT equality kernel, never a positive full-space eigenvalue margin. The
numerical formulation below uses the full matrix without a kernel quotient.

## 2. Lossless intrinsic primal: explicitly restore a minimizer

For arbitrary finite sample jets obeying every Q_ij<=0 and the GD recursion,
there is a legal smooth convex function with a minimizer and the SAME V_h.
This justifies the intrinsic model at the level of actual functions as well
as the preceding full-certificate column argument.

Fix epsilon>0 and append one coordinate. Put

 g'_i=(g_i,epsilon), x'_i=(x_i,T-epsilon P_i),
 f'_i=f_i+c-epsilon^2 P_i.

The GD recursion remains exact, and direct substitution shows Q'_ij=Q_ij:
the extra function difference -epsilon^2(P_i-H_j) cancels the extra
position term epsilon^2(P_i-H_j). Choose the finite constants

 c>=max_i{-f_i+epsilon^2 P_i+(||g_i||^2+epsilon^2)/2},
 epsilon T>=max_i{f_i+c-<g_i,x_i>+(||g_i||^2+epsilon^2)/2}.

After adding the optimum jet (0,0,0), these are exactly Q'_(star,i)<=0
and Q'_(i,star)<=0. The full conjugate interpolation construction in
accepted fact1252b8823bb5a290 supplies an everywhere finite convex
1-smooth interpolant with this optimum and every displayed jet. Thus it
is a genuine same-function GD run with an actual minimizer.

The additional V contribution is epsilon^2 times

 2sum_i h_i(s-P_i)+sum_i h_i(1-h_i)-s(s+1)=0,

because 2sum_i h_i(s-P_i)=s^2+sum_i h_i^2. Hence V'_h=V_h exactly.
This lift does NOT preserve the original distance or normalized objective,
and is used ONLY for intrinsic primitive validity. Zero steps and n=0
cause no exception; identical original queries stay identical after a zero
step. All formal original gradients remain in the final cone model.

It follows that minimizing V_h over the full Gram G_prim>=0, trace(G_prim)=1, all
ordered Q_ij<=0, and a harmless value gauge f_n=0 detects primitive failure
exactly at the sign level. Every negative feasible V yields a legal failure
by the lift. Conversely every nonzero-gradient legal failure can be divided
by its positive total squared gradient to meet trace(G_prim)=1. Zero-gradient
jets cannot have negative V, since all values then agree. A common-gradient
Huber transcript has V=0, so this minimum is always <=0.

For positive steps the primal also has strict feasibility: use a diagonal
quadratic with n+1 distinct curvatures in (0,1) and nonzero initial
coordinates, then scale to trace(G_prim)=1. The gradient polynomial columns
z product_(t<i)(1-h_t z) have distinct degrees 1,...,n+1 and are independent
at n+1 nonzero distinct nodes (divide by z). Every finite distinct-query
residual is strictly negative. This is the same Vandermonde argument as in
accepted1252b8823bb5a290. Values are bounded after fixing f_n because the
two ordered residuals bound every f_i-f_n and trace(G_prim)=1 bounds every Gram
entry. Thus no unbounded objective is concealed by the gauge.

## 3. Composition identities, including arbitrary PSD child remainders

These identities are rederived from the Q definition and coincide with the
proofs of Zhang--Jiang, arXiv:2410.12395v2, Theorems3.1/3.2. Their exact
local paper, definitions, and both proofs were reread. No external result
with unexamined hypotheses is needed for the algebra below.

Primitive join. Let a,b be primitive, A=1+sum a, C=1+sum b. Let d>0 solve

 d(A+C+d)=AC; T=A+C+d, kappa=(T/C)^2.

Form h=(a,1+d,b). Let p be the bridge query and z the terminal query, and
let j range over the nonterminal queries in b. Direct expansion gives

 V_h=V_a+kappa V_b-(kappa-1)[sum_j b_j Q_(j,p)+Q_(z,p)
                                  +d Q_(p,z)+sum_j b_j Q_(j,z)]. (3)

One way to audit all coefficients is to call the bracket C_aux; its value
part is 2sum_j b_j f_j+(d-C)f_p+(2-C-d)f_z, and its gradient part is

 sum_j b_j(1-b_j)||g_j||^2+[(C-d)/2-d^2]||g_p||^2
                                          +(C+d)||g_z||^2/2.

Insert these in (3), and use (kappa-1)(C-d)=2(A+d), kappa d^2=A^2,
kappa C^2=T^2. The mixed terms cancel. Since C>d and kappa>1, every
bracket coefficient is nonnegative. Child certificates with matrices M_a,
M_b produce the FULL parent remainder M_a+kappa M_b on disjoint original
child-gradient sets. They may have arbitrary PSD rank. Its mass is

 T=Omega(A,C)=(A+C+sqrt((A+C)^2+4AC))/2.

Dominant join. Define, for weights u_i>=0 of mass D=2sum h+1,

 J_(h,u)=||x_0||^2+2sum_i u_i Q_(i,star)-2D f_z
                              -||x_0-sum_i u_i g_i||^2.

Dominance means J>=0 on every legal run; it implies W(h)<=1/D by Q_i*<=0.
The empty word has u=1 and J=0. A primitive word is dominant with
u=(h,s+1), because expanding the last square gives J=V_h exactly.
For a primitive left child of mass A, and a dominant right child of mass D
and weights v, put

 mu=2A/D, r=(1+sqrt(1+8A/D))/2, gamma=A+1, beta=1+A/r.

The parent word is (a,beta,b). Its weights are u=(a,gamma,r v), all
nonnegative, of total G(A,D)=2A+D/2+sqrt(D^2+8AD)/2=Dr^2. Its identity is

 J_parent=V_a+r^2 J_right-2mu sum_(i>p) v_i Q_(i,p).             (4)

To verify the equality expand both sides. The value flow uses
mu D=(sum a)+gamma=2A. The gradient coefficients reduce to
(sum a)(sum a+1)+mu D+gamma=gamma^2,
r^2=r+mu, and r^2 beta=mu+gamma r; these cancel every mixed term and
leave the defining parent square. This proves (4), including empty
children. It proves exactly the ConPD rule in accepted benchmark notation.

## 4. A finite seed threshold that compares the ORIGINAL exact B_n

Let lambda=1+sqrt(2), rho=log_2(lambda), q=1/rho. Suppose an actual
nonnegative primitive word a has length r_seed and A_seed=1+sum a. Put
m_seed=r_seed+1. The sufficient target condition is

                       A_seed>m_seed^rho.                      (5)

A gain merely above C_(r_seed) does not imply (5).
Here are the full amplification argument and the parameter losses.
First Omega(x,y)^q<=x^q+y^q for x,y>=0. For positive arguments use
x=exp(u-t), y=exp(u+t), t>=0. It becomes J(t)>=0 where

 J(t)=q^(-1)log(2cosh(qt))-arsinh(cosh t).

J(0)=0 and J(t) tends to zero at infinity. Its derivative has the sign
of qt-alpha(t), where alpha=artanh(sinh(t)/sqrt(1+cosh(t)^2)). Direct
differentiation gives alpha'=cosh(t)/sqrt(1+cosh(t)^2), strictly increasing
from 1/sqrt(2) to1. Also 1/sqrt(2)<q<1: lambda>2 and lambda^3<16 imply
1>q>3/4>1/sqrt(2). Thus alpha-qt starts by decreasing, has strictly
increasing derivative, and tends to infinity; it crosses zero once on
t>0. Therefore J first increases and then decreases to zero, proving the
inequality. Zero arguments follow directly from Omega(x,0)=x.

Induction on each primitive binary tree now gives C_k<= (k+1)^rho.
Unrolling a maximizing dominant tree, its primitive side branches have
leaf counts l_1,...,l_t summing to n. Since G(A,D)<=D+4A,

 B_n<=1+4sum l_i^rho<=1+4n^rho.

Balanced primitive trees give C_(2^k-1)=lambda^k, by the preceding upper
bound and Omega(A,A)=lambda A. Hence B_(2^k)>=G(lambda^k,1)>=2lambda^k.
The number K=limsup B_n/n^rho thus satisfies 2<=K<=4. No phase limit is
assumed and this is the ORIGINAL exact DP sequence.

Take a fully expanded optimizing dominant tree at length n, with smallest
maximizing split as deterministic tie rule. It has exactly n primitive
empty leaves and one final dominant empty leaf: induction on the unique
right dominant chain proves this count. Replace every primitive empty
leaf by a and recompute all physical bridges by (3)-(4). Leave the final
dominant empty leaf unchanged. The actual resulting word Lift_n has length
m_seed n and is dominant. Homogeneity of Omega makes each primitive mass
exactly A_seed times its former mass.

Let D_n=2sum Lift_n+1. In an auxiliary scalar evaluation also change the
terminal dominant mass from1 to A_seed. Homogeneity of G makes that
auxiliary root A_seed B_n. Accepted0be8b54e0a33381c proves
sqrt(G(A,d))=(sqrt(d)+sqrt(d+8A))/2 and that this is increasing and
1-Lipschitz as a function of sqrt(d). Propagating the unchanged terminal
leaf discrepancy therefore gives

 sqrt(A_seed B_n)-(sqrt(A_seed)-1)<=sqrt(D_n)<=sqrt(A_seed B_n). (6)

Choose n_j with B_(n_j)/n_j^rho tending to K. Equation(6) gives
D_(n_j)/n_j^rho tending to A_seed K, whereas by the SAME global limsup
limsup B_(m_seed n_j)/(m_seed n_j)^rho<=K. Consequently

 limsup_j B_(m_seed n_j) W_(m_seed n_j)(Lift_(n_j))
                           <= m_seed^rho/A_seed <1.            (7)

For delta=(1-m_seed^rho/A_seed)/2>0, every sufficiently large output
horizon in this subsequence has ratio <=1-delta. This is a fixed relative
all-function upper separation and suffices to refute the original uniform
asymptotic matching claim, conditional on the actual certified seed.
The unused dominant leaf and its O(sqrt(B_n)) mass loss are explicit.

## 5. Before-solve design, calibration signs, and scope

The new discovery lane searches length15 only, with every coordinate free
and fixed capacity A_target=(1+eta)lambda^4, initially eta=1/1000. The
known Silver word has length15 and exactly lambda^4 capacity, so (5) has
zero baseline threshold gap. Source order, unequal step magnitudes, and the
full all-pair sensitivity guide proposals; no symmetry or tensor class is
imposed. Length4 is used only for two exact model calibrations.

Calibration expected signs BEFORE any solve:
* h=(1,2,1,1) has min V=0. Its nonzero lambda entries are 01:2,10:4,
  21:3,31:3,41:3,14:3,23:8,32:16,34:35,43:48,24:3. Expansion gives
  M=|g0-g1|^2+4|g2-g3|^2+16|g3-g4|^2. Huber attains0.
* h=(3,1,1,1) is nonprimitive. Use x=(1,-2,-3/2,-1,-1/2),
  g=(1,-1/2,-1/2,-1/2,-1/2), f_i=g_i x_i-g_i^2/2. This is the
  convex1-smooth function with derivative clipped to[-1/2,1]. Direct
  substitution gives V=-45/4 and sum||g_i||^2=2. The trace-one model
  therefore has min V<=-45/8. Every actual query and optimum Q<=0.
* The full exact length15 Silver word has min V=0 by (3), starting with
  empty leaves. Its full nonnegative array is generated by (3), with
  identically zero PSD remainder. Its e kernel is not made strict.

The primal objective is NOT normalized objective error. Only an exactly
reconstructed nonnegative certificate satisfying (1) and (5), followed by
(3)-(7), could settle the original negative route. Solver feasibility,
positive residual tolerances, clipped eigenvalues, or a finite capacity
gain below (5) do not do so. All original statuses and raw primal/dual
arrays must be retained. The allowance is at most72 evaluations,120 measured
build-plus-solve seconds,n<=15,40 distinct nonbaseline words. No old budget
is renewed and no verifier call is made.

## 6. C74: a full-array source rigidity lemma and its local scope

This appendix is part of the C72 joint draft. It is UNVERIFIED. It is not a
proof that an improved primitive seed is impossible. The numerical normal
at the end is not an exact PSD certificate.

Define the balanced primitive words Sil_0=(), Sil_(m+1)=(Sil_m,1+lambda^(m-1),Sil_m),
where lambda=1+sqrt(2). Thus Sil_m has length2^m-1 and capacity lambda^m.
The complete array Lambda_m is obtained from (3) starting with the empty
array, and has identically zero full remainder. It is the UNIQUE full
certificate even if all incoming-terminal coefficients are allowed to be
signed, while every other coefficient is nonnegative. Here and below
"certificate" includes cancellation of all values and PSD on all original
gradients. The optimum columns are already forced zero by Section1.

Here is a full proof rather than an assumption of a unique optimizer.
At a root write h=(a,1+d,b), A=1+sum a, C=1+sum b, T=A+C+d, dT=AC,
kappa=(T/C)^2, eta=d/C. Let p be the bridge query, z the final query,
P={queries through p}, and B={queries after p through z}.
There are two legal equality runs. A far-out unit-slope Huber ray has all
gradients1 and all Q=V=0. The clipped derivative f'(x)=min(1,max(x,-eta)),
with f(0)=0 and initial x_0=A, has gradients1 on P and -eta on B. If
R_i^a is the remaining left step sum before p and R_j^b the remaining
right sum before z, its finite data are

 x_i=1+R_i^a, f_i=R_i^a+1/2       (i in P),
 x_j=-eta(1+R_j^b), f_j=eta^2(R_j^b+1/2) (j in B).

Its same-group residuals vanish and its cross residuals are exactly

 Q_(i,j)=-(1+eta)eta R_j^b, Q_(j,i)=-(1+eta)R_i^a.             (8)

Moreover f_i=g_i x_i-g_i^2/2 throughout, so telescoping gives
V=A^2-T^2 eta^2=0. Every incoming-terminal Q is zero. Therefore even with
signed incoming-terminal multipliers, evaluating any supplied PSD
certificate on these runs gives zero, and every nonterminal negative-Q
edge has zero coefficient. Positivity of all source steps leaves only
cross edges i->z (i in P) and j->p (j in B).

Write their coefficients nu_i and mu_j respectively. Both equality
gradient vectors lie in the PSD kernel; they span the block indicators
of P and B. The full mixed form, before using those kernels, is

 -sum_(i in P) nu_i <g_i,sum_(j in B,j<z)b_j g_j+g_z>
                        +d sum_(j in B)mu_j <g_p,g_j>.

Rows i<p of the matrix applied to the B indicator force C nu_i=0.
Rows in B applied to the P indicator then force
mu_j=nu_p b_j/d for j<z and mu_z=nu_p/d. Summing value-flow equations
through P gives sum mu-nu_p=2(A+d). Hence

 nu_p=d(kappa-1), mu_j=(kappa-1)b_j, mu_z=kappa-1.              (9)

In particular every full mixed coefficient is zero. Formula(3) then
splits the supplied full matrix as M_a+kappa M_b, on disjoint original
gradient blocks, where the prefix array is unchanged and the tail array is

 lambda^b_ij=lambda_ij/kappa
                 -(1-1/kappa)b_i 1_(i<z,j=z).

Both blocks are PSD. The prefix array is ordinary; the tail may have
signed terminal coefficients but all other signs remain nonnegative.
Thus induction may be applied inside both balanced children. The base
empty word has the zero array. For the one-step base, writing u=sqrt(2),
value cancellation gives lambda_10=lambda_01+2u, while the constant
kernel forces lambda_01=2; its matrix is (2-u^2)|g0-g1|^2=0.
The induction with (9) uniquely reconstructs Lambda_m, and forces all
full child and parent matrices to be zero. This proves the stated rigidity.
It is rigidity at this exact word, not a neighborhood or global theorem.

## 7. Exact continuation identity away from a saturated cut

For ANY positive a,b and bridge1+d with 0<d<C=1+sum b, put
A=1+sum a, T=A+C+d, and now define

 kappa=1+2(A+d)/(C-d),
 R=(A+d)(AC-dT)/(C-d).

No primitive assumption and no saturation equation is used. Direct
coefficient comparison, retaining every value and gradient, yields

 V_h=V_a+kappa V_b-(kappa-1)C_aux
                              +R(|g_p|^2-|g_z|^2),             (10)

where C_aux is the same bracket in(3). The values match because
(kappa-1)(C-d)=2(A+d). The unmatched squared-gradient coefficients are
A^2-kappa d^2 and kappa C^2-T^2; their sum is zero and the first equals
R. This verifies all terms using the explicit expansion in Section3.

Recursively use(10) on the fixed complete binary tree near Sil_m. Every
actual coefficient in the resulting array Lambda(h) is nonnegative,
since d>0,C>d,kappa>1 persist. Its FULL remainder is the diagonal form

 sum_(nodes v) w_v R_v (|g_(p(v))|^2-|g_(z(v))|^2),             (11)

where w_v is the product of kappa along those ancestors at which the
node lies in the right subtree. This is an exact formal identity, NOT
a PSD claim: away from the source its diagonal terms need not be positive.
It uses no fixed-remainder, rank, or symmetry assumption about OTHER
certificates that might exist for the same changed word.

At the source Sil_m, let a node have depth d_v (root depth0) and r_v right
turns among its ancestors. Its two child capacities are
A_v=lambda^(m-d_v-1), its bridge gap is A_v/lambda, and w_v=lambda^(2r_v).
For an arbitrary first variation u of ALL the physical steps,

 dR_v[u]=sqrt(2) A_v [sum_(steps in left or right child)u_i
                                             -2lambda u_(p(v))]. (12)

To check this derivative, the factor AC-dT vanishes at the source, and
(A+d)/(C-d)=lambda. Its derivative is
(C-d)dA+(A-d)dC-(T+d)dd, giving(12). No derivative of w_v contributes
because R_v=0 at the source.

## 8. A concrete exact normal-completion interface

Consider any full finite jet (G_prim,f) for the actual source Sil_m, where G_prim is
the Gram matrix of its2^m original gradients, satisfying

 G_prim>=0, Q_ij<=0 for EVERY ordered finite pair,
 Q_ij=0 on EVERY positive entry of Lambda_m.                    (13)

All quantities Q here use the source word and the independent f_i;
a common value shift may set the last value to zero. Suppose for some
c_tan>0 its diagonals further satisfy the explicit2^m-1 equations

 G_(p(v),p(v))-G_(z(v),z(v))
             = c_tan lambda^(2d_v-m-2r_v)/(2^(d_v+1)sqrt(2)).       (14)

Then the envelope sensitivity of the COMPLETE canonical array is exactly

                  S(G_prim,f)=-c_tan(1,...,1).                         (15)

Proof: differentiate (2) along the explicitly defined array Lambda(h)
in(11). Every derivative of a multiplier is supported where Lambda_m>0,
so its contribution vanishes on(13). By(12), put
 t_v=w_v sqrt(2)A_v(G_pp-G_zz). Equation(14) says
 t_v=c_tan lambda^(d_v-1)/2^(d_v+1).
For the step at a node of depth d the resulting sensitivity is
sum_(proper ancestors v)t_v-2lambda t_node. The geometric sum is
c_tan[(lambda/2)^d-1], because lambda/2=1+1/(2lambda).
The second term is c_tan(lambda/2)^d, so their difference is -c_tan, proving(15).
This yields the sensitivity without assuming favorable cross-gradient
terms: the ENTIRE matrix and all-Q legality remain obligations in(13).

If a solution of(13)-(14) is actually proved, every sequence of fully
certifiable words h_l tending to Sil_m obeys

 limsup_l [sum(h_l)-sum(Sil_m)]/||h_l-Sil_m|| <=0,                 (16)

with zero differences omitted. Thus a C^1 primitive-certificate path out
of this source cannot have a positive first-order capacity derivative.
Here is a proof that retains arbitrary re-selection of all coefficients.
A fixed sufficiently small-curvature quadratic, run with nearby positive
words, has every finite Q strictly negative, uniformly in a small
neighborhood: choose theta so all theta h_i<1 and use x_0=1. Its iterates
are positive and distinct; Q_ij=-theta(1-theta)(x_i-x_j)^2/2.
Evaluating a certificate on this actual run bounds the sum of ALL its
nonnegative coefficients by a common constant. Hence along any sequence
of nearby certificates, compactness gives convergent subsequences.
Every limit is a full source certificate and therefore equals Lambda_m
by Section6. Thus every selected array tends to Lambda_m.

Evaluate the nearby PSD remainder on the fixed G_prim from(13). At the
source, V=0 by the zero remainder and complementarity. The unchanged
source part is sum lambda_l Q(source)<=0: positive source coefficients
multiply zero, and all other coefficients are nonnegative. The change
with h therefore bounds the nonnegative remainder above by

 S(G_prim,f) dot(h_l-Sil_m)+o(||h_l-Sil_m||).

This follows directly from the polynomial V and Q expressions, the
convergence lambda_l->Lambda_m, and their uniform boundedness. Equation(15)
now gives(16). This uses no derivative or pseudoinverse of the selected
lambda_l and no strict full-space remainder margin.

Crucial scope: even(16) would NOT prohibit a positive capacity gain of
second or higher order along a curved path, nor a distant seed. It does
not prove C_(2^m-1) is globally optimal over primitive schedules. No such
normal is asserted to exist merely because its finite SDP appears feasible.
For an explicit logical scope check, the set {(x,y):y<=x^2} has tangent
cone y<=0 at(0,0), but contains(t,t^2) with positive y for every t!=0.
Indeed for convergent normalized directions the inequality gives the
y-component<=0, whereas t^2/sqrt(t^2+t^4) tends to zero. Thus a first-order
normal alone cannot exclude higher-order objective gains in a nonconvex
feasible set. This example is not asserted to be a GD schedule.

The all-depth algebra makes the missing construction specific. In a parent
normal of strength c_tan, the required left/right child normal strengths are
c_tan_left=(lambda/2)c_tan and c_tan_right=c_tan/(2lambda). The root also needs
G_pp-G_zz=c_tan/(2sqrt(2)lambda^m). Cross-child Q and full PSD must be realized
simultaneously at those exact scales. Independent child scaling without
this original-space completion proves nothing. At length15 this is one
16-gradient Gram completion with fixed diagonal differences, not an
asymptotic benchmark comparison or a surrogate finite objective gain.

## 9. Counted finite discovery and its limits

The full primitive model used all240 ordered finite inequalities and the
complete16-gradient Gram matrix. At the exact source, its canonical
remainder is zero. Two bounded all-coordinate sensitivity probes completed
at capacity ratios1.001 and1.0001 above lambda^4; no feasible improved seed
was found. The best reported energies were -0.06933970529217337 and
-0.006501508090531515, respectively. Both those best outputs had status
`optimal`; other statuses, all residuals, primal/dual arrays and exact
source versions are retained. These are finite non-discovery observations,
not exact nonprimitive proofs or a theorem about arbitrary schedules.

The counted auxiliary evaluation44, at the SAME known baseline word Sil_4,
maximized c_tan under(13), trace(G_prim)=1, and the full sensitivity equations(15).
It reported `optimal`, c_tan=1.0908231587401278, max equality error below1e-14,
and smallest full Gram eigenvalue approximately -3.47e-11. That last value
is NEGATIVE; nothing was clipped and no PSD certificate is claimed.
Raw file: research/max3_full_primitive_discovery_raw/044.json in the public
project research directory. Exact reconstruction of(13)-(14), and then a
second-order or nonlocal analysis if such a normal is proved, is the next
analytic interface. No source-face numerical output is promoted to a fact.

All numerical work in this wave stops at44 calls and26.517837785992016
measured build-plus-solve seconds, with39 distinct nonbaseline words.
This includes five calibration calls (two inaccurate), two initial search
calls (one inaccurate and one user_limit), sixteen1.001 proposals,
twenty1.0001 proposals, and the single auxiliary normal call. The old closed672 plus this worker's44 calls total716; independently
allocated sibling calls are not included in that subtotal. The latest
main guidance f719b26e39837b65 records an additional sibling allocation

# max3 round18 handoff: C79 discovery and C80 exact obstruction

Status: original target OPEN; no four-step full-cone seed. C78 is accepted fact04219554e41d9750 with its conditional scope. C79/C80 are UNVERIFIED; no new fact_submit, installation or subagent was used.

This is the first round of the eighth launch, cumulative18. One already-authorized analytical round remains under cap19; no model-round or numerical allowance is renewed.

## Mathematical output

C79 derives the full primitive energy/flow/PSD identity, the exact constant-gradient kernel, two-way zero-step certificate compression, a legal cut filter and curvature polynomial. The actual numerical models retain all20 multipliers and all5 gradient variables; the kernel dual imposes only Me=0. All four step coordinates and total sum were free, with sum>=168/25. Eight bounded adaptive starts included different orders, zero/subunit endpoints and larger total masses. Starts failing necessary filters were still counted and their full models evaluated. This is finite non-discovery, not a proof of global optimality or seed impossibility.

C80 gives one explicit globally convex1-smooth triangle-projection function. For h=(1450,2269,1656,1345)/1000, sum h=168/25, every primitive single-cut test is strict and EVERY convex quadratic passes (32 exact positive Bernstein coefficients for V(z)/z^3), yet V=-2741319415267/2821081730000<0. This rejects that exact word through an actual legal function. Full normals, true closest-minimizer distance, original Gram/values/all20Q and the exact polynomial are preserved.

For ANY adjacent b,c>1 in a nonnegative word, put P=1+prefix sum,B=b-1,C=c-1,D=1+suffix sum,U=b+c+suffix sum,A=1+total sum,E=c+suffix sum+1,delta=PE-AB. Primitive feasibility necessarily requires P²D>=BC[P²+2BP+BU], equivalently CE-D<=C(P+B)delta/P². If D<=BC, an actual one-sided capped quadratic gives V=P²-(P+bc)²<0; if D>BC, the triangle proves the inequality. This is not a global saturation assumption or a sufficient criterion.

Shared mathematical findings: bf740a9c1acf0955 (fixed exact counterexample), 5f9459ff7c5dc786 (quantitative cut defect), 7b447329aac48edd (complete adjacent-pair regime). Complete proof: max3_c80_triangle_obstruction.md.

## Numerical evidence and accounting failure

There were99 actual conic solves (45 full primal,54 full kernel dual),89 optimal and10 optimal_inaccurate outputs. One additional exact rational candidate operation is record99. All100 original raw record hashes match their ledger entries; no raw value/status was changed. Calibration2 stopped on optimal_inaccurate; its charged replay3 returned optimal. Calibration source word and explicit certificates are archived.

Best recorded kernel_dual point passing saved necessary screens at tolerance1e-7: record34, h=[1.686285353839162, 1.4325949755034117, 2.252870251447667, 1.3482494192097587], normalized score=-0.005304649538003198, original value=-0.3161486250257297. This remains negative and is not a feasible certificate.
Best recorded primal point passing saved necessary screens at tolerance1e-7: record71, h=[1.4506015067457587, 2.269659477150598, 1.653033924002999, 1.3467050921006436], normalized score=-0.004701121489724665, original value=-0.28017931899320647. This remains negative and is not a feasible certificate.

An accounting audit disclosed25 fixed words considered by hand but omitted from pre-proposal registration. All25 are conservatively appended as charges101..125 and listed in max3_four_step_manual_proposal_audit.json. Corrected total:125 charged evaluations/128,120 distinct nonbaseline words/96, proposal overrun24, measured machine build-plus-solve time1.8549624290899374 seconds. No machine optimization ran for the25 manual records and no CPU time is invented. This cap overrun is an actual workflow failure. The ledger is CLOSED with statusclosed_budget_audit_overrun_no_further_discovery; all further numerical work is stopped and the3 unused charges expire. Closed earlier ledgers were not modified. Shared disclosure:6f57ab3197b4cc9d.

## Precise remaining work

The full four-step seed existence problem remains open over all original variables. C80 excludes a scalar-filter sufficiency shortcut and provides an additional directed polynomial inequality, but is not a complete separator. The separately saved rational h=(8/5,2,471/250,309/250) passes the unit-quadratic/cut/three adjacent tests in hand calculations and still had negative full numerical primal value -0.9385750496312056 (record100); no exact certification of that screening claim or rejection is asserted. Its saved Gram suggests a different planar pattern with four distinct gradients and g3=g4, so an exact construction retaining function-value offsets is a concrete next analytical direction. Do not treat solver rank, scalar screens, or any unverified follow-up as a fact.

# C79: four-step full-cone discovery and exact interfaces

Status: UNVERIFIED joint research draft. Wave8, cumulative round18.
No fact_submit is authorized. All new algebra below is derived jointly;
older shared drafts are orientation, never correctness premises.
The accepted interpolation fact1252b8823bb5a290 is the only fact used.

## Primitive energy and the full cone

Normalize smoothness to1. For a nonnegative word h of length n, put
s=sum h_i, A=s+1, P_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, T_i=sum_(t>i)h_t and
x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t. Define

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
 V_h=2sum_(i<n)h_i(f_i-f_n)+sum_(i<n)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
                                      -s(s+1)||g_n||^2.

Let Lambda be the full nonnegative array on distinct finite indices0..n,
with zero diagonal. Set e=(1,...,1)^T, L=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda,
T_ii=1, T_it=h_t for t<i, T_it=0 for t>i, and

 omega=(-2h_0,...,-2h_(n-1),2s)^T,
 D=diag(h_0(2-h_0),...,h_(n-1)(2-h_(n-1)),-s(s+2)).

Expansion of the value coefficients gives L^T e=omega, and expansion of
the gradient coefficients gives

 V_h+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g)>,
 M=D+(LT+T^T L^T)/2.                                      (1)

For example the position part of sum Lambda Q has matrix
sym(L(T-I)); the squared-gradient differences contribute
sym(L)-diag(omega)/2. Adding the displayed diagonal of V gives (1).
Consequently the original full interface is exactly flow, Lambda>=0 and
M>=0. It is sufficient for V>=0 on every actual run, by Q_ij<=0 from
accepted1252b8823bb5a290. Every original finite gradient is retained.

Flow alone forces e^T M e=0: Te has entries1+P_i (P_n=s), and
e^T LTe=omega^T Te=s^2+sum h_i^2, whereas
e^T De=-s^2-sum h_i^2. PSD therefore forces Me=0. Indeed for any v,
positivity of (e+t v)^T M(e+t v) for both signs of small t forces
v^T Me=0. Thus the exact dual formulation may impose Me=0 and maximize
t subject to M-t(I-ee^T/(n+1))>=0. Its t>=0 feasibility is precisely the
original cone. This removes only the proved constant-gradient kernel;
no quadratic equality, rank, ordering, symmetry or sparsity is assumed.

The companion full primal minimizes V subject to Gram(g)>=0,
trace(Gram(g))=1, f_n=0 and every original ordered Q<=0. Its value is
always <=0: a constant-gradient Huber run gives V=0. Every negative
feasible transcript can be lifted to a legal function with a minimizer.
To see this, append the coordinate
g'_i=(g_i,epsilon), x'_i=(x_i,R-epsilon P_i),
f'_i=f_i+c-epsilon^2 P_i, with epsilon>0. Then Q'_ij=Q_ij.
Choose finite c and R so

 c>=max_i(-f_i+epsilon^2 P_i+(||g_i||^2+epsilon^2)/2),
 epsilon R>=max_i(f_i+c-<g_i,x_i>+(||g_i||^2+epsilon^2)/2).

These inequalities add the zero optimum jet in both orientations. The
conjugate construction of accepted1252b8823bb5a290 supplies the actual
convex1-smooth function. The additional V is epsilon^2 times
2sum h_i(s-P_i)+sum h_i(1-h_i)-s(s+1)=0, since
2sum h_i P_i=s^2-sum h_i^2. No distance-preservation claim is made.

## Zero steps: exact two-way cone correspondence

Partition the query indices into maximal consecutive classes separated
by positive steps. In each class except the last, choose the index of
the positive outgoing step as representative; choose n in the last.
Let hhat be the ordered list of positive steps. Embedding any compressed
certificate on these representatives gives the original V and Q on
their independent formal gradients. All omitted coefficients are zero,
so its full matrix is the coordinate-selection congruence of the
compressed PSD matrix. This proves the forward embedding.

Conversely, restrict an original certificate to formal gradients and
values that are equal within each class. Its V becomes V_hhat; each
cross-class Q becomes the corresponding compressed Q; each within-class
Q is zero. Sum all original nonnegative multipliers on each ordered
pair of distinct classes. This yields compressed nonnegative multipliers,
the compressed flow, and a PSD matrix by congruence. Thus certificate
existence is equivalent in both directions, including all-zero schedules
(V=0, Lambda=0). No numerical model below uses this compression: all
five original gradients are still saved and optimized.

## Necessary filters proved from legal functions

For j with b=h_j>1, write P=P_j, R=T_j and a=(b-1)/(R+1)>0.
Use the one-dimensional convex1-smooth function whose derivative is
clip(x,-a,1), with minimum0, and start at x_0=P+1. Every query through
j is on the upper plateau, the bridge lands at -(R+1)a, and all later
queries remain on the lower plateau until x_n=-a. At every query
f_i=x_i g_i-g_i^2/2, equivalently Q_(i,star)=0. Summing the GD norm
identity and substituting this equality into V yields

 V_h=(P+1)^2-A^2 a^2.

Thus a necessary condition for the cone is

 A(h_j-1)_+ <= (P_j+1)(T_j+1), for every j.                (2)

For h_j<=1 this is automatic. At j=0 or n-1, (2) gives
h_j<=2(s+1)/(s+2)<2 when h_j>1. Also, for h_j>1,
h_j-1 <=(P_j+1)(T_j+1)/A<=min(P_j+1,T_j+1), because
A=P_j+T_j+h_j+1 >=max(P_j+1,T_j+1).
At n4 this bounds h_1,h_2<4 using the adjacent endpoint bound.
So [0,2]x[0,4]x[0,4]x[0,2] is a lossless closed outer box.
It does not imply all points of the box, or all points satisfying (2),
are primitive. In particular no upper sum below12 is claimed.

For curvature z in(0,1], use f(x)=z x^2/2, x_0=1, and
p_0(z)=1, p_(i+1)(z)=(1-z h_i)p_i(z). Direct substitution gives

 V_h(z)=sum_i z h_i(1+z-z h_i)p_i(z)^2
                      -s z[1+(s+1)z]p_n(z)^2 >=0.        (3)

At z=1 this becomes 1-A^2 product_i(1-h_i)^2>=0 by telescoping.
Each fixed z gives a necessary test only. The implementation records
z=1,1/2,1/4; it performs no hidden curvature or word scan.

## Exact calibrations

The word (1,2,1,1) is the ConPP join of (1) and (1,1): their masses are
2 and3, the bridge increment d=1 solves d(2+3+d)=2*3, and the physical
bridge is1+d=2. Independently of any external composition theorem, its
complete nonzero certificate entries are

 01:2, 10:4, 21:3, 31:3, 41:3, 14:3,
 23:8, 32:16, 34:35, 43:48, 24:3.

Substitution in (1) gives exactly
M=(e_0-e_1)(e_0-e_1)^T+4(e_2-e_3)(e_2-e_3)^T
                               +16(e_3-e_4)(e_3-e_4)^T.
The child certificates are lambda01=2,lambda10=4,M=(e0-e1)^2
for (1), and lambda01=2,lambda10=4,lambda12=8,lambda21=12,
M=(e0-e1)^2+4(e1-e2)^2 for (1,1).
Thus the exact primitive primal minimum is0, attained by constant jets.

For (3,1,1,1), use x=(1,-2,-3/2,-1,-1/2),
g=(1,-1/2,-1/2,-1/2,-1/2), f_i=x_i g_i-g_i^2/2.
These are samples of the same clipped-gradient function with slopes
[-1/2,1]. They give V=-45/4 and sum g_i^2=2, hence the full primal
minimum is at most -45/8. All20 original interpolation inequalities
and both optimum orientations are obeyed. This fixes the expected sign.

An additional exact length4 zero-remainder ConPP calibration is built
recursively from the empty word: join children a,b of masses A,C using
d>0 satisfying d(A+C+d)=AC, k=((A+C+d)/C)^2, and h=(a,1+d,b).
For bridge query p and terminal query z, the exact identity is

 V_h=V_a+k V_b-(k-1)[sum_(j in b nonterminal)b_j Q_(j,p)
                 +Q_(z,p)+d Q_(p,z)+sum_j b_j Q_(j,z)].  (4)

It follows by expanding Q and using (k-1)(C-d)=2(A+d),
k d^2=A^2 and k C^2=(A+C+d)^2; C>d>0 gives every actual sign.
Each recursive child retains all its original indices. The baseline
uses a=join(empty,empty) and b=join(empty,a), then join(a,b).
The primitive calibration is (1), (4) and its explicit multiplier array,
not an ordinary objective-gap claim. Source terminology was checked in
Zhang--Jiang, arXiv2410.12395v2, Definition2.7 and Theorem3.1 proof;
the displayed identities are independently rederived here.

## Numerical scope and accounting

The authorized new ledger is max3_four_step_seed_discovery_budget.json:
128 charged model/optimization evaluations,120 measured build-plus-solve
seconds,96 distinct nonbaseline proposals,n4 only. Every proposal is
registered before its evaluation, including filtered or failed proposals;
all original data and source hashes are saved. Old closed ledgers are
neither imported as executable runners nor modified.

The search uses all four physical step coordinates with s>=168/25,
the lossless box, necessary (2)-(3), and full primal/dual sensitivities.
Several distant initializations are explored adaptively; neither the sum
nor the rank nor the order is fixed as a global reduction. A local
non-discovery is not global nonexistence. Numerical near-zero, success
status, a clipped eigenvalue or PSD repair is never an exact seed.
Any actual result must satisfy the original flow, all20 signs, full PSD
and sum threshold exactly; C78's separate audit must also be checked.

# C80: an exact two-dimensional four-step primitive obstruction

Status: UNVERIFIED joint candidate, no verifier allocation. This tests a
specific full primitive interface, not the original asymptotic theorem.
The defining construction and every required normal are given below.

Let h=(a,b,c,d), with a,d>=0, b>1, c>1. Define

 P=a+1, B=b-1, C=c-1, D=d+1, U=b+c+d, A=1+a+b+c+d.

Assume D>BC and put

 Kappa=B^2 C+U(D-BC), t=B^2 C/Kappa.

Then Kappa>0 and0<t<1. In the Euclidean plane define

 u=(1,0), w=(t,sqrt(t(1-t))), v=-(B u+D w)/c,
 K=conv(u,v,w), F(x)=max_(g in K)(<x,g>-||g||^2/2).

Here K is a nondegenerate triangle. Since B u+c v+D w=0 and B,c,D>0,
zero is in its interior. Completing the square shows grad F=P_K,
the Euclidean projection onto K. For completeness, the projection normal
inequalities at two points imply
||P_K(x)-P_K(y)||^2<=<P_K(x)-P_K(y),x-y>, so the projection is1-Lipschitz.
The unique-maximizer formula gives
0<=F(x+z)-F(x)-<P_K(x),z><=<P_K(x+z)-P_K(x),z><=||z||^2,
proving differentiability. F is convex as a supremum of affine functions.
Since0 is interior to K, for every x!=0 sufficiently small epsilon>0
has epsilon x in K, whence F(x)>=epsilon(1-epsilon/2)||x||^2>0.
Thus0 is its unique minimizer and F(0)=0.

The actual iterates and gradients are

 x=(P u,u,-B u,D w,w), g=(u,u,v,w,w).                    (1)

The four GD updates follow from c v=-B u-D w and D=d+1.
It remains to check that each listed gradient is the actual projection.
The inner products are

 ||u||^2=1, <u,w>=||w||^2=t,
 <u,v>=-(B+D t)/c, <v,w>=-(B+D)t/c.

At x0 the residual x0-u=a u has nonpositive pairing with v-u and w-u;
at x1 the residual is zero. At x2 the residual y2=-B u-v satisfies

 <w-v,y2>=[-B^2(c-1)+Kappa t]/c^2=0,
 <u-v,y2>=-B(1-t)(c-1)/c<0.

The first equality uses the expansion
Kappa=D(2B+D)+c(D-BD+B)-B c^2
       =B^2(c-1)+(b+c+d)(D-B(c-1)).
At x3 the residual d w pairs to zero with u-w and to
-d t[1+(B+D)/c]<=0 with v-w. At x4 the residual is zero.
These are all vertex comparisons and establish every projection normal.

Put f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2. Then for every original ordered pair

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2
      =<g_i-g_j,x_j-g_j><=0.

The optimum comparisons hold because0 belongs to K. In fact Q_(i,star)=0
for all i. There is one explicit globally legal function, with true initial
distance P to its unique minimizer, and no missing terminal comparison.

For the intrinsic energy

 V_h=2sum_(i=0)^3 h_i(f_i-f_4)+sum_(i=0)^3 h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
                                            -s(s+1)||g_4||^2,

where s=A-1, summing the GD norm identities and using
f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2 gives

 V_h=||x0||^2-||x4||^2-2s f4-s(s+1)||g4||^2
     =P^2-A^2 t.                                       (2)

Any original full nonnegative interpolation/PSD certificate must therefore
obey the following additional necessary inequality in this regime:

 P^2 D >= BC[P^2+2BP+BU].                               (3)

Indeed A=P+U and
A^2 B^2C-P^2 Kappa=U{BC[P^2+2BP+BU]-P^2D}, so (2)>=0
is equivalent to (3). This is only a necessary condition for b,c>1 and
D>BC; it is not a full characterization or a global capacity obstruction.

## Fixed rational specialization

One proposed exact word, selected by rounding the geometry of saved
discovery record71, is

 h=(1450,2269,1656,1345)/1000, sum h=168/25.              (4)

The single charged fixed-candidate operation is record99 in
max3_four_step_seed_discovery_raw/099.json. It contains every original
rational Gram entry, function value and ordered Q. It gives exactly

 t=66024801/564216346, D-BC=189067/125000,
 V_h=-2741319415267/2821081730000<0.

The exact full trace-normalized energy is
-504402772409128/1718090401421875. Thus (4) cannot admit the full
primitive certificate. No numerical PSD repair, clipping, solver rank
assumption or scan is used in this proof.

Nevertheless, all four primitive single-cut tests hold strictly: their
slacks (1+prefix)(1+tail)-A(h_j-1), in chronological order, are

 699/250, 577/100000, 1200347/200000, 9279/2500.          (5)

The word also passes EVERY convex1-smooth scalar quadratic, not just the
three numerical screening curvatures. To show this exactly, for curvature
z in[0,1] put p0=1, p_(i+1)=(1-z h_i)p_i. Direct substitution gives

 V_h(z)=sum_i z h_i(1+z-z h_i)p_i^2
                            -s z[1+(s+1)z]p4^2=z^3 R(z).

Writing R(z)=sum_(i=0)^7 r_i z^i, the exact coefficients r0..r7 are

 293892535459/1000000000,
 -2565070268077021/1000000000000,
 25501482292887241/2500000000000,
 -9335047522883723193/400000000000000,
 4094864133735484813551/125000000000000000,
 -175057366829794377658881/6250000000000000000,
 52516866551109429450429/3906250000000000000,
 -54411016852014358527117/19531250000000000000.

Here is a finite exact positive-basis certificate for R. For l=0,1/4,1/2,3/4,
expand R(l+x/4)=sum_(k=0)^7 beta_(l,k) binom(7,k)x^k(1-x)^(7-k).
The rational coefficient formula is

 beta_(l,k)=sum_(j=0)^k [binom(k,j)/binom(7,j)] 4^(-j)
                                sum_(i=j)^7 r_i binom(i,j)l^(i-j). (6)

The four rows of coefficients are explicitly:

 l=0:
 293892535459/1000000000,
 5663920724774979/28000000000000,
 118466995993825981/840000000000000,
 89350661850225432007/896000000000000000,
 80179881684171436848551/1120000000000000000000,
 7019131829546593320971719/134400000000000000000000,
 1084787387282508694745631/28000000000000000000000,
 9355453517805011570547413/320000000000000000000000.

 l=1/4:
 9355453517805011570547413/320000000000000000000000,
 22096679133334733204006651/1120000000000000000000000,
 47709397590871145509381441/3360000000000000000000000,
 6015731198047189848729817/560000000000000000000000,
 1318803156277689465811/156250000000000000000,
 716661139723887953927849/105000000000000000000000,
 395098446318964178528277/70000000000000000000000,
 23757859952262975297321/5000000000000000000000.

 l=1/2:
 23757859952262975297321/5000000000000000000000,
 270121632344399129796711/70000000000000000000000,
 48818671114313258247593/15000000000000000000000,
 24439962963628753248587/8750000000000000000000,
 1364515762711357617321817/560000000000000000000000,
 1030893661419515971816909/480000000000000000000000,
 305805648670522563365657/160000000000000000000000,
 548894926580824071855791/320000000000000000000000.

 l=3/4:
 548894926580824071855791/320000000000000000000000,
 121544638955150754245067/80000000000000000000000,
 654595436858189642563771/480000000000000000000000,
 346215797597421765010013/280000000000000000000000,
 78984917611546674402131/70000000000000000000000,
 7602563302701652626313/7500000000000000000000,
 431841029282863888083/546875000000000000000,
 176902547823562831/39062500000000000000.

Every displayed numerator and denominator is positive. The Bernstein
basis is nonnegative and sums to1 on[0,1], so R>0 on each of these four
closed subintervals. Thus V_h(z)>0 for every0<z<=1, and V_h(0)=0.
An arbitrary positive-semidefinite quadratic of smoothness at most1
decomposes into its scalar eigencoordinates; V is additive across these
coordinates. Hence the word passes the entire convex quadratic class.

Equations(1)--(6) prove a concrete scope limitation: even strict primitive
single-cut tests plus ALL scalar/multidimensional quadratics do not
suffice for the four-step full cone at exactly the required mass.
This says nothing about the existence of another four-step seed.

## Closing the complementary adjacent-pair regime

If b,c>1 but D<=BC, use instead the one-dimensional function with
derivative min(x,1) and minimum value0 at its unique minimizer0.
Its formula is F(x)=x^2/2 for x<=1 and F(x)=x-1/2 for x>=1.
The iterates from x0=P are

 x1=1, x2=-B, x3=BC, x4=BC-D+1>=1,
 g=(1,1,-B,1,1).

All four physical updates hold. Again Q_(i,star)=0, while
f4=x4-1/2, so the exact primitive energy is

 V_h=P^2-(x4+s)^2=P^2-(P+bc)^2<0.                      (10)

Thus D>BC is itself necessary for a primitive word when b,c>1.
Combining (10) with the triangle regime proves that (3), equivalently
(9), is a necessary condition for EVERY adjacent b,c>1, without a
separate assumption on D-BC. Its positive right side also implies
D>BC directly. This is still not a condition on pairs having a step<=1.

## Additional adjacent-pair filter

The same construction applies to any nonnegative word with two adjacent
steps b>1,c>1. Let a be the sum of all preceding steps and d the sum of
all following steps. Use initial point(a+1)u, retain gradient u throughout
the preceding steps, and retain w throughout the following steps.
All intermediate residuals on those blocks are nonnegative multiples of
u or w and satisfy the already displayed normals. Thus (3) is a valid
necessary condition at each adjacent pair with b,c>1, without asserting
that the prefix or suffix is primitive: the complementary one-dimensional
construction also retains constant gradients throughout those blocks.
In particular it may
be applied separately at j=0,1,2 of the original four-step word.

There is also an exact quantitative relation to the single-cut slack.
Put E=c+d+1=C+D+1 and delta=P E-A B, which is the single-cut
slack at b. Since A=P+B+E, direct expansion gives

 P^2 D-BC[P^2+2BP+BU]
        =P^2(D-CE)+C(P+B)delta.                         (7)

Thus the same actual triangle yields the exact energy

 V_h=U[P^2(D-CE)+C(P+B)delta]/Kappa.                     (8)

In particular primitive feasibility in the stated regime forces

 CE-D <= C(P+B)delta/P^2.                               (9)

At a saturated cut(delta=0), (9) forces the two-step suffix cut
condition CE<=D. For positive cut slack it gives its explicit allowable
defect, without assuming a primitive prefix or suffix. This is a
quantitative local interface, not an assertion that all capacity-optimal
words have a saturated cut or that the remaining global problem closes.

If an actual full-cone seed is later found, accepted04219554e41d9750 supplies
the conditional asymptotic lift. No such seed is supplied here, and this
new obstruction has not been submitted to the verifier.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000730 -->
# C81: a four-step triangle with an interior-edge query

Status: complete new joint UNVERIFIED construction. No verifier call is
allocated. This is analytical continuation, cumulative round19/19, of
the CLOSED C79 discovery allocation. No optimization, candidate sweep,
or exact numerical replay is performed here. The only fixed schedule
specialized below is the already saved record100.

The accepted fact04219554e41d9750, Section10, supplies the original
primitive energy and its full certificate identity. All new geometry and
energy calculations below are proved directly rather than imported from
the unverified C79/C80 drafts.

## 1. General actual function and four-step trajectory

Let h=(a,b,c,d), with a>1, b>1, c>1, d>=0. Define

 B=b-1, C=c-1, D=d+1, s=a+b+c+d, A=1+s.

Choose u>0 and z>0 satisfying

 u>=B,
 z^2 <= (u+1)((a-1)u-1),
 z^2 >= C d(D-BC)/D^2.                                  (1)

The second inequality implies (a-1)u-1>0. The third imposes
no additional restriction if its right side is negative. Put

 p=(u,0), q=(-1,z), r=(-BC/D,0), K=conv{p,q,r},
 F(x)=max_(g in K) {<x,g>-||g||^2/2}.                      (2)

This nondegenerate compact triangle contains0 in the relative interior
of its horizontal base. Completing the square shows that the unique
maximizer in (2) is the Euclidean projection P_K(x).

For completeness, if p_x=P_K(x), p_y=P_K(y), the two projection
normal inequalities give

 ||p_x-p_y||^2 <= <p_x-p_y,x-y>.

Hence P_K is1-Lipschitz. The maximizing formula gives

 0<=F(x+t)-F(x)-<p_x,t>
       <=<P_K(x+t)-p_x,t><=||t||^2,

so F is differentiable with gradient P_K; it is convex as a supremum
of affine functions. Since0 belongs to K, F>=0 and F(0)=0. Moreover
P_K(x)=0 exactly when x is in the normal cone of K at0. Pairing x
with both signs of the horizontal base forces its first coordinate
to vanish; pairing it with q then forces its second coordinate<=0.
Thus argmin F={(0,t):t<=0}.

The proposed ORIGINAL iterates and gradients are

 x0=(a u-1,z),       g0=(u,0),
 x1=(-1,z),         g1=(-1,z),
 x2=(B,-Bz),        g2=(B,0),
 x3=(-BC,-Bz),      g3=(-BC/D,0),
 x4=(-BC/D,-Bz),    g4=(-BC/D,0).                        (3)

The four equations x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i g_i follow by subtraction.
The initial point has positive second coordinate, so its closest
minimizer is0 and its squared distance is(a u-1)^2+z^2.

We check every required projection normal, including both terminal
orientations implicitly through the single actual function.

At x0, the residual y0=x0-g0=((a-1)u-1,z) has

 <q-p,y0>=z^2-(u+1)((a-1)u-1)<=0,
 <r-p,y0>=(-BC/D-u)((a-1)u-1)<0.

At x1 the residual is0. At x2, g2 is on the base because
-BC/D<0<B<=u, and y2=x2-g2=(0,-Bz) has zero pairing with
both base directions and pairing-Bz^2<0 with q-g2.

At x3 the residual is y3=(-BCd/D,-Bz). Its base comparison
with p-r is nonpositive, and

 <q-r,y3>=B{C d(D-BC)/D^2-z^2}<=0.

At x4 the residual y4=(0,-Bz) has zero pairing with the base
and pairing-Bz^2<0 with q-r. These are all vertices of K,
so (3) gives the actual gradient of the same F at every query.

For all five indices define f_i=F(x_i)=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2.
Every original ordered interpolation residual has the exact form

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2
     =<g_i-g_j,x_j-g_j><=0.                              (4)

This follows from the proved projection normals. The optimum sample0
also obeys all ordered comparisons. Thus no guessed rank or deleted
gradient is needed to certify legality.

## 2. Exact intrinsic energy and a necessary family of inequalities

Use the energy from accepted04219554e41d9750, Section10:

 V_h=2sum_(i=0)^3 h_i(f_i-f4)
       +sum_(i=0)^3 h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-s(s+1)||g4||^2.

The identity

 ||x_i||^2-||x_(i+1)||^2
     =2h_i f_i+h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2

follows from (3) and f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2.
Summing it gives

 V_h=||x0||^2-||x4||^2-2s f4-s(s+1)||g4||^2.

Here f4=||g4||^2/2, ||x4||^2=(BC/D)^2+B^2z^2, and therefore

 V_h=(a u-1)^2+(1-B^2)z^2-A^2(BC/D)^2.                 (5)

Consequently the original full primitive cone must satisfy

 (a u-1)^2+(1-B^2)z^2 >= A^2(BC/D)^2                  (6)

for EVERY u,z obeying(1). Indeed its accepted exact identity is
V_h+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g)>, where Lambda_ij>=0,
the flow holds, and M is PSD in all five original gradients.
The right side is nonnegative and every Q_ij<=0, so it implies V_h>=0.
A negative value in(5) contradicts this identity directly.

This is a necessary family, not a sufficient condition. The imposed
a,b,c>1 is a scope assumption of the witness and is NOT a lossless
restriction of the full four-step search domain.

## 3. Exact rejection of saved discovery record100

Keep the already proposed schedule

 h=(8/5,2,471/250,309/250),   s=168/25,   A=193/25.

Thus B=1,C=221/250,D=559/250. Choose u=2,z=3/5.
The upper inequality in(1) is

 z^2=9/25 <=3/5=(u+1)((a-1)u-1).

For its lower inequality,

 C d(D-C)/D^2
    =(221*309*338)/(250*559^2) < 9/25,

because 221*309*338=23081682 <28123290=90*559^2.
All numbers in(2)--(4) are therefore rational. Explicitly,

 K=conv{(2,0),(-1,3/5),(-221/559,0)},

 x0=(11/5,3/5),   g0=(2,0),
 x1=(-1,3/5),     g1=(-1,3/5),
 x2=(1,-3/5),     g2=(1,0),
 x3=(-221/250,-3/5), g3=(-221/559,0),
 x4=(-221/559,-3/5), g4=(-221/559,0).

The exact values, if desired independently of(4), are

 f0=12/5, f1=17/25, f2=1/2,
 f3=(d+1/2)(221/559)^2, f4=(221/559)^2/2.

Equation(5), with B=1, gives

 V_h=(11/5)^2-(193*221/(25*559))^2
    =-(11908*73398)/(625*559^2)<0.                        (7)

The comparison is exact:193*221=42653>30745=55*559.
The initial closest-minimizer distance squared is26/5. The terminal
gradient/value and the displayed negative energy concern the intrinsic
primitive criterion. They do not assert an objective minimax theorem.

This rejects the precise candidate left in the round18 handoff.
The handoff's numerical rank/value observations were only motivation;
no numerical Gram, tolerance, PSD repair or approximate identity is a
premise of this proof. No new schedule has been proposed or evaluated.

## 4. Exact one-parameter elimination when b=2

For1<a<=2, b=2, c>1 and d>0 with D>C, put

 L_81=C d(D-C)/D^2>0,
 u_81=[2-a+sqrt(a^2+4(a-1)L_81)]/[2(a-1)].

The function H(u)=(u+1)((a-1)u-1) is strictly increasing for
u>=1/(a-1); its derivative there is at least a>0. Its positive
root for H(u)=L_81 is exactly u_81. Because L_81>0,
u_81>1/(a-1)>=1. Hence u=u_81,z=sqrt(L_81) is allowed in(1).
Every full primitive certificate in this regime therefore forces

 a u_81-1 >= A C/D.                                    (8)

The left side is positive, so(8) is equivalent to(6) in this
particular specialization. This explicitly eliminates the witness
parameters; it is not a complete classification of b=2 schedules,
let alone of all four-step schedules.

## 5. Retrieval and correctness boundary

Before proving, fact_search found the accepted conditional bridge and
the general interpolation fact1252b8823bb5a290, but no accepted version
of(1)--(8). Shared-memory search found C80 and older projection-lift
drafts, whose claims are not used as facts here.

The required Matlas query returned three unrelated descent results;
none is used. The existing exact local copy of Taylor--Hendrickx--Glineur,
arXiv1502.05666, Sections2.4--2.5 and the proof of Theorem4, was reread
for the conjugate construction. All needed projection/differentiability
claims are proved above, so this candidate imports no external theorem.

The original four-step existence question and original asymptotic
target remain OPEN. Accepted04219554e41d9750 gives the conditional lift
if a different actual full seed is found. No fact_submit is authorized
or performed for this draft.

and pairing-Bz^2<0 with q-r. These are all vertices of K,
so (3) gives the actual gradient of the same F at every query.

For all five indices define f_i=F(x_i)=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2.
Every original ordered interpolation residual has the exact form

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2
     =<g_i-g_j,x_j-g_j><=0.                              (4)

This follows from the proved projection normals. The optimum sample0
also obeys all ordered comparisons. Thus no guessed rank or deleted
gradient is needed to certify legality.

## 2. Exact intrinsic energy and a necessary family of inequalities

Use the energy from accepted04219554e41d9750, Section10:

 V_h=2sum_(i=0)^3 h_i(f_i-f4)
       +sum_(i=0)^3 h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-s(s+1)||g4||^2.

The identity

 ||x_i||^2-||x_(i+1)||^2
     =2h_i f_i+h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2

follows from (3) and f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2.
Summing it gives

 V_h=||x0||^2-||x4||^2-2s f4-s(s+1)||g4||^2.

Here f4=||g4||^2/2, ||x4||^2=(BC/D)^2+B^2z^2, and therefore

 V_h=(a u-1)^2+(1-B^2)z^2-A^2(BC/D)^2.                 (5)

Consequently the original full primitive cone must satisfy

 (a u-1)^2+(1-B^2)z^2 >= A^2(BC/D)^2                  (6)

for EVERY u,z obeying(1). Indeed its accepted exact identity is
V_h+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g)>, where Lambda_ij>=0,
the flow holds, and M is PSD in all five original gradients.
The right side is nonnegative and every Q_ij<=0, so it implies V_h>=0.
A negative value in(5) contradicts this identity directly.

This is a necessary family, not a sufficient condition. The imposed
a,b,c>1 is a scope assumption of the witness and is NOT a lossless
restriction of the full four-step search domain.

## 3. Exact rejection of saved discovery record100

Keep the already proposed schedule

 h=(8/5,2,471/250,309/250),   s=168/25,   A=193/25.

Thus B=1,C=221/250,D=559/250. Choose u=2,z=3/5.
The upper inequality in(1) is

 z^2=9/25 <=3/5=(u+1)((a-1)u-1).

For its lower inequality,

 C d(D-C)/D^2
    =(221*309*338)/(250*559^2) < 9/25,

because 221*309*338=23081682 <28123290=90*559^2.
All numbers in(2)--(4) are therefore rational. Explicitly,

 K=conv{(2,0),(-1,3/5),(-221/559,0)},

 x0=(11/5,3/5),   g0=(2,0),
 x1=(-1,3/5),     g1=(-1,3/5),
 x2=(1,-3/5),     g2=(1,0),
 x3=(-221/250,-3/5), g3=(-221/559,0),
 x4=(-221/559,-3/5), g4=(-221/559,0).

The exact values, if desired independently of(4), are

 f0=12/5, f1=17/25, f2=1/2,
 f3=(d+1/2)(221/559)^2, f4=(221/559)^2/2.

Equation(5), with B=1, gives

 V_h=(11/5)^2-(193*221/(25*559))^2
    =-(11908*73398)/(625*559^2)<0.                        (7)

The comparison is exact:193*221=42653>30745=55*559.
The initial closest-minimizer distance squared is26/5. The terminal
gradient/value and the displayed negative energy concern the intrinsic
primitive criterion. They do not assert an objective minimax theorem.

This rejects the precise candidate left in the round18 handoff.
The handoff's numerical rank/value observations were only motivation;
no numerical Gram, tolerance, PSD repair or approximate identity is a
premise of this proof. No new schedule has been proposed or evaluated.

## 4. Exact one-parameter elimination when b=2

For1<a<=2, b=2, c>1 and d>0 with D>C, put

 L_81=C d(D-C)/D^2>0,
 u_81=[2-a+sqrt(a^2+4(a-1)L_81)]/[2(a-1)].

The function H(u)=(u+1)((a-1)u-1) is strictly increasing for
u>=1/(a-1); its derivative there is at least a>0. Its positive
root for H(u)=L_81 is exactly u_81. Because L_81>0,
u_81>1/(a-1)>=1. Hence u=u_81,z=sqrt(L_81) is allowed in(1).
Every full primitive certificate in this regime therefore forces

 a u_81-1 >= A C/D.                                    (8)

The left side is positive, so(8) is equivalent to(6) in this
particular specialization. This explicitly eliminates the witness
parameters; it is not a complete classification of b=2 schedules,
let alone of all four-step schedules.

## 5. Retrieval and correctness boundary

Before proving, fact_search found the accepted conditional bridge and
the general interpolation fact1252b8823bb5a290, but no accepted version
of(1)--(8). Shared-memory search found C80 and older projection-lift
drafts, whose claims are not used as facts here.

The required Matlas query returned three unrelated descent results;
none is used. The existing exact local copy of Taylor--Hendrickx--Glineur,
arXiv1502.05666, Sections2.4--2.5 and the proof of Theorem4, was reread
for the conjugate construction. All needed projection/differentiability
claims are proved above, so this candidate imports no external theorem.

The original four-step existence question and original asymptotic
target remain OPEN. Accepted04219554e41d9750 gives the conditional lift
if a different actual full seed is found. No fact_submit is authorized
or performed for this draft.


# C82: exact elimination of the C81 witness parameters

Status: UNVERIFIED joint extension. For the complete construction and
proof, concatenate the C81 proof with this file. C81 is not an accepted
fact; its geometry is part of the joint proof, not an imported premise.
There are no new word proposals, optimizer calls or numerical replays.

Let h=(a,b,c,d), a,b,c>1,d>=0, and put

 alpha=a-1, B=b-1, C=c-1, D=d+1, A=1+a+b+c+d,
 H(u)=(u+1)(alpha u-1),
 ell=max{0,C d(D-BC)/D^2},
 R=[2-a+sqrt(a^2+4alpha ell)]/(2alpha),
 U=max{B,R}, K0=A^2(BC/D)^2.

Define three scalar coefficients

 q2=a^2+(1-B^2)alpha,
 q1=-2a+(1-B^2)(a-2),
 q0=B^2-K0,

and q(u)=q2 u^2+q1 u+q0.

## 1. Feasible parameter domain

The C81 triangle has parameters u>=B,z>0 with

 ell<=z^2<=H(u).

Since alpha>0, H has its only nonnegative root at1/alpha;
H'(u)=2alpha u+a-2>=a>0 for u>=1/alpha.
Solving H(u)=ell therefore gives R>=1/alpha exactly as above.
The closure of the admissible parameter domain is

 u>=U, ell<=z^2<=H(u).                                  (1)

Every boundary point in(1) with z=0 is a limit of points with
u>U,z>0 obeying the original normal inequalities. In particular
H(u)>ell for u>U. Taking such a limit in the explicit polynomial
energy is justified by continuity; no assertion about strict normals
at the limiting point is needed.

## 2. Closed formula for the infimum over this entire witness family

The energy is

 E(u,z)=(a u-1)^2+(1-B^2)z^2-K0.

If0<B<=1, its smallest value at fixed u uses z^2=ell.
Moreover a u-1>0 throughout u>=U>=1/alpha, so that value is
strictly increasing in u. Its infimum over the family is

 E_min=(a U-1)^2+(1-B^2)ell-K0.                         (2)

If B>1, its smallest value at fixed u uses z^2=H(u).
Expanding the resulting expression gives exactly q(u). Hence:

 * If q2<0, or q2=0 and q1<0, then E_min=-infinity.
 * If q2=0 and q1>=0, then E_min=q(U).
 * If q2>0, set u_opt=max{U,-q1/(2q2)}. Then
   E_min=q(u_opt).                                      (3)

These statements follow respectively from the leading term, from
linearity on the half-line, and from completing the square
q(u)=q2(u+q1/(2q2))^2+q0-q1^2/(4q2).

Every original full primitive certificate with a,b,c>1,d>=0
therefore must satisfy E_min>=0, with E_min defined by(2)--(3).
If E_min<0, an actual finite u and z>0 give a negative energy:
for an attained strict negative value this follows directly or by
continuity from a zero-z boundary; for E_min=-infinity take a
sufficiently large finite u. The C81 normals then give an actual
convex1-smooth function with a minimizer. This is a completely
parameter-eliminated necessary test for this particular family.

## 3. A broad monotone regime

Suppose1<a<=2 and1<b<=1+sqrt(3). If B<=1, formula(2)
already applies. If1<B<=sqrt(3), then

 q2>=a^2-2(a-1)=(a-1)^2+1>0,
 q'(1/alpha)=a[2/alpha+1-B^2]>=0,

because alpha<=1 and B^2<=3. Since q2>0, q' increases;
thus q is nondecreasing throughout u>=U>=1/alpha.
Consequently no stationary-point alternative is needed in this
regime: E_min=q(U) if B>1. If additionally R>=B, then H(U)=ell
and BOTH cases reduce to the same explicit radical inequality

 (a R-1)^2+(1-B^2)ell >= A^2(BC/D)^2.                    (4)

At B=1 it specializes to Section4 of C81. These are conditional
parameter statements. No unverified global-domain reduction is used
to claim that an arbitrary four-step seed lies in this regime.

## 4. Exact scope of the remaining gap

Eliminating the witnesses is not eliminating the original twenty
nonnegative multipliers. The displayed tests are necessary because
each negative case yields an actual trajectory; their nonnegative
cases certify only that this particular triangle family cannot reject
the schedule. No converse to the full flow/PSD interface is proved.
In particular failure to derive a contradiction from(2)--(4) does not
give a seed, and a negative saved optimization score does not give a
global impossibility theorem.
